- **relative-abundance-accuracy-assessment**: Use when planning metatranscriptome sequencing or judging whether a sample's depth suffices for accurate abundance measurement, especially of low-abundance transcripts in gut microbiome mRNA data. - **relative-abundance-calculation**: Use when after generating a frequency count table (e.g., from count_fold_changes output showing numbers of increased vs. decreased metabolites by class) and before creating proportional visualizations (pie charts, stacked bar plots). - **relative-abundance-comparison-across-profilers**: Use when benchmarking CAMI-style taxonomic profiles where single-value metrics (F1 score, L1 norm, UniFrac) look equivalent across profilers, and you need to see which clades at class/phylum rank drive abundance differences versus ground truth.
